Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on lie_groups structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 236. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 760 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on lie_groups structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 236. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 760. QED.
